<h1 id="overseerr-api">Overseerr API v1.0.0</h1>

> Scroll down for code samples, example requests and responses. Select a language for code samples from the tabs above or the mobile navigation menu.

This is the documentation for the Overseerr API backend.

Two primary authentication methods are supported:

- **Cookie Authentication**: A valid sign-in to the `/auth/plex` or `/auth/local` will generate a valid authentication cookie.
- **API Key Authentication**: Sign-in is also possible by passing an `X-Api-Key` header along with a valid API Key generated by Overseerr.

Base URLs:

* <a href="http://localhost:5055/api/v1">http://localhost:5055/api/v1</a>

# Authentication

* API Key (cookieAuth)
    - Parameter Name: **connect.sid**, in: cookie.

* API Key (apiKey)
    - Parameter Name: **X-Api-Key**, in: header.

<h1 id="overseerr-api-public">public</h1>

Public API endpoints requiring no authentication.

## Get Overseerr status

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/status \
  -H 'Accept: application/json'
```

`GET /status`

Returns the current Overseerr status in a JSON object.

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "commitTag": "string",
  "updateAvailable": true,
  "commitsBehind": 0,
  "restartRequired": true
}
```

<h3 id="get-overseerr-status-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Returned status|Inline|

<h3 id="get-overseerr-status-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|version|string|false|none|none|
|commitTag|string|false|none|none|
|updateAvailable|boolean|false|none|none|
|commitsBehind|number|false|none|none|
|restartRequired|boolean|false|none|none|

<aside class="success">
This operation does not require authentication
</aside>

## Get application data volume status

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/status/appdata \
  -H 'Accept: application/json'
```

`GET /status/appdata`

For Docker installs, returns whether or not the volume mount was configured properly. Always returns true for non-Docker installs.

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "appData": true,
  "appDataPath": "/app/config"
}
```

<h3 id="get-application-data-volume-status-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Application data volume status and path|Inline|

<h3 id="get-application-data-volume-status-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|appData|boolean|false|none|none|
|appDataPath|string|false|none|none|

<aside class="success">
This operation does not require authentication
</aside>

<h1 id="overseerr-api-settings">settings</h1>

Endpoints related to Overseerr's settings and configuration.

## Get main settings

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/main \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /settings/main`

Retrieves all main settings in a JSON object.

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "apiKey": "string",
  "appLanguage": "en",
  "applicationTitle": "Overseerr",
  "applicationUrl": "https://os.example.com",
  "trustProxy": true,
  "csrfProtection": false,
  "hideAvailable": false,
  "partialRequestsEnabled": false,
  "localLogin": true,
  "newPlexLogin": true,
  "defaultPermissions": 32
}
```

<h3 id="get-main-settings-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|OK|[MainSettings](#schemamainsettings)|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Update main settings

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/main \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "appLanguage": "en",
    "applicationTitle": "Overseerr",
    "applicationUrl": "https://os.example.com",
    "trustProxy": true,
    "csrfProtection": false,
    "hideAvailable": false,
    "partialRequestsEnabled": false,
    "localLogin": true,
    "newPlexLogin": true,
    "defaultPermissions": 32
  }'
```

`POST /settings/main`

Updates main settings with the provided values.

> Body parameter

```json
{
  "appLanguage": "en",
  "applicationTitle": "Overseerr",
  "applicationUrl": "https://os.example.com",
  "trustProxy": true,
  "csrfProtection": false,
  "hideAvailable": false,
  "partialRequestsEnabled": false,
  "localLogin": true,
  "newPlexLogin": true,
  "defaultPermissions": 32
}
```

<h3 id="update-main-settings-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|[MainSettings](#schemamainsettings)|true|none|
|» appLanguage|body|string|false|none|
|» applicationTitle|body|string|false|none|
|» applicationUrl|body|string|false|none|
|» trustProxy|body|boolean|false|none|
|» csrfProtection|body|boolean|false|none|
|» hideAvailable|body|boolean|false|none|
|» partialRequestsEnabled|body|boolean|false|none|
|» localLogin|body|boolean|false|none|
|» newPlexLogin|body|boolean|false|none|
|» defaultPermissions|body|number|false|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "apiKey": "string",
  "appLanguage": "en",
  "applicationTitle": "Overseerr",
  "applicationUrl": "https://os.example.com",
  "trustProxy": true,
  "csrfProtection": false,
  "hideAvailable": false,
  "partialRequestsEnabled": false,
  "localLogin": true,
  "newPlexLogin": true,
  "defaultPermissions": 32
}
```

<h3 id="update-main-settings-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Values were sucessfully updated|[MainSettings](#schemamainsettings)|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

|body|body|[PlexSettings](#schemaplexsettings)|true|none|

|» port|body|number|true|none|
|» useSsl|body|boolean \| null|false|none|
|» webAppUrl|body|string \| null|false|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "name": "Main Server",
  "machineId": "1234123412341234",
  "ip": "127.0.0.1",
  "port": 32400,
  "useSsl": true,
  "libraries": [
    {
      "id": "string",
```

## Get Plex libraries

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/plex/library \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /settings/plex/library`

Returns a list of Plex libraries in a JSON array.

<h3 id="get-plex-libraries-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|sync|query|string \| null|false|Syncs the current libraries with the current Plex server|
|enable|query|string \| null|false|Comma separated list of libraries to enable. Any libraries not passed will be disabled!|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
[
  {
    "id": "string",
    "name": "Movies",
    "enabled": false
  }
]
```

<h3 id="get-plex-libraries-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Plex libraries returned|Inline|

<h3 id="get-plex-libraries-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|*anonymous*|[[PlexLibrary](#schemaplexlibrary)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Get status of full Plex library scan

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/plex/sync \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /settings/plex/sync`

Returns scan progress in a JSON array.

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "running": false,
  "progress": 0,
  "total": 100,
  "currentLibrary": {
    "id": "string",
    "name": "Movies",
    "enabled": false
  },
  "libraries": [
    {
      "id": "string",
      "name": "Movies",
      "enabled": false
    }
  ]
}
```

<h3 id="get-status-of-full-plex-library-scan-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Status of Plex scan|Inline|

<h3 id="get-status-of-full-plex-library-scan-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|running|boolean|false|none|none|
|progress|number|false|none|none|
|total|number|false|none|none|
|currentLibrary|[PlexLibrary](#schemaplexlibrary)|false|none|none|
|libraries|[[PlexLibrary](#schemaplexlibrary)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Start full Plex library scan

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/plex/sync \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "cancel": false,
    "start": false
  }'
```

`POST /settings/plex/sync`

Runs a full Plex library scan and returns the progress in a JSON array.

> Body parameter

```json
{
  "cancel": false,
  "start": false
}
```

<h3 id="start-full-plex-library-scan-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|object|false|none|
|» cancel|body|boolean|false|none|
|» start|body|boolean|false|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "running": false,
  "progress": 0,
  "total": 100,
  "currentLibrary": {
    "id": "string",
    "name": "Movies",
    "enabled": false
  },
  "libraries": [
    {
      "id": "string",
      "name": "Movies",
      "enabled": false
    }
  ]
}
```

<h3 id="start-full-plex-library-scan-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Status of Plex scan|Inline|

<h3 id="start-full-plex-library-scan-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|running|boolean|false|none|none|
|progress|number|false|none|none|
|total|number|false|none|none|
|currentLibrary|[PlexLibrary](#schemaplexlibrary)|false|none|none|
|libraries|[[PlexLibrary](#schemaplexlibrary)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Gets the user's available Plex servers

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/plex/devices/servers \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /settings/plex/devices/servers`

Returns a list of available Plex servers and their connectivity state

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
[
  {
    "name": "My Plex Server",
    "product": "Plex Media Server",
    "productVersion": "1.21",
    "platform": "Linux",
    "platformVersion": "default/linux/amd64/17.1/systemd",

    "lastSeenAt": "2021-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    "provides": [
      "server"
    ],
    "owned": true,
    "ownerID": "12345",
    "home": true,
    "sourceTitle": "xyzabc",
    "accessToken": "supersecretaccesstoken",
    "publicAddress": "127.0.0.1",
    "httpsRequired": true,
    "synced": true,
    "relay": true,
    "dnsRebindingProtection": false,
    "natLoopbackSupported": false,
    "publicAddressMatches": false,
    "presence": true,

      {
        "protocol": "https",

        "uri": "https://127-0-0-1.2ab6ce1a093d465e910def96cf4e4799.plex.direct:32400",
        "local": true,
        "status": 200,

      }
    ]
  }
]
```

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/plex/users \

  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /settings/plex/users`

## Create Radarr instance

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/radarr \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "name": "Radarr Main",
    "hostname": "127.0.0.1",
    "port": 7878,
    "apiKey": "exampleapikey",
    "useSsl": false,
    "baseUrl": "string",
    "activeProfileId": 1,
    "activeProfileName": "720p/1080p",
    "activeDirectory": "/movies",
    "is4k": false,
    "minimumAvailability": "In Cinema",
    "isDefault": false,
    "externalUrl": "http://radarr.example.com",
    "syncEnabled": false,
    "preventSearch": false
  }'
```

`POST /settings/radarr`

Creates a new Radarr instance from the request body.

> Body parameter

```json
{
  "name": "Radarr Main",
  "hostname": "127.0.0.1",
  "port": 7878,
  "apiKey": "exampleapikey",
  "useSsl": false,
  "baseUrl": "string",
  "activeProfileId": 1,
  "activeProfileName": "720p/1080p",
  "activeDirectory": "/movies",
  "is4k": false,
  "minimumAvailability": "In Cinema",
  "isDefault": false,
  "externalUrl": "http://radarr.example.com",
  "syncEnabled": false,
  "preventSearch": false
}
```

<h3 id="create-radarr-instance-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|[RadarrSettings](#schemaradarrsettings)|true|none|
|» name|body|string|true|none|
|» hostname|body|string|true|none|
|» port|body|number|true|none|
|» apiKey|body|string|true|none|
|» useSsl|body|boolean|true|none|
|» baseUrl|body|string|false|none|
|» activeProfileId|body|number|true|none|

## Modify job schedule

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/jobs/string/schedule \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "schedule": "0 */5 * * * *"
  }'
```

## Batch update all sliders.

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/settings/discover \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '[
    {
      "id": 1,
      "type": 1,
      "title": "string",
      "isBuiltIn": true,
      "enabled": true,
      "data": "1234"
    }
  ]'
```

`POST /settings/discover`

Batch update all sliders at once. Should also be used for creation. Will only update sliders provided
and will not delete any sliders not present in the request. If a slider is missing a required field,
it will be ignored. Requires the `ADMIN` permission.


> Body parameter

```json
[
  {
    "id": 1,
    "type": 1,
    "title": "string",
    "isBuiltIn": true,
    "enabled": true,
    "data": "1234"
  }
]
```

<h3 id="batch-update-all-sliders-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|[[DiscoverSlider](#schemadiscoverslider)]|true|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
[
  {
    "id": 1,
    "type": 1,
    "title": "string",
    "isBuiltIn": true,
    "enabled": true,
    "data": "1234"
  }
]
```

<h3 id="batch-update-all-sliders-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Returned all newly updated discovery sliders|Inline|

<h3 id="batch-update-all-sliders-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|*anonymous*|[[DiscoverSlider](#schemadiscoverslider)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

```json
  "id": 1,
```

```json
  "plexToken": "string",

  "permissions": 0,
  "avatar": "string",
```

## Sign in using a Plex token

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/auth/plex \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -d '{
    "authToken": "string"
  }'
```

`POST /auth/plex`

Takes an `authToken` (Plex token) to log the user in. Generates a session cookie for use in further requests. If the user does not exist, and there are no other users, then a user will be created with full admin privileges. If a user logs in with access to the main Plex server, they will also have an account created, but without any permissions.

> Body parameter

```json
{
  "authToken": "string"
}
```

<h3 id="sign-in-using-a-plex-token-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|object|true|none|
|» authToken|body|string|true|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "id": 1,
  "email": "hey@itsme.com",
  "username": "string",
  "plexToken": "string",
  "plexUsername": "string",
  "userType": 1,
  "permissions": 0,
  "avatar": "string",
  "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
  "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
  "requestCount": 5
}
```

<h3 id="sign-in-using-a-plex-token-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|OK|[User](#schemauser)|

<aside class="success">
This operation does not require authentication
</aside>

## Update batch of users

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/user \
  -X PUT \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "ids": [
      0
    ],
    "permissions": 0
  }'
```

`PUT /user`

Update users with given IDs with provided values in request `body.settings`. You cannot update users' Plex tokens through this request.

Requires the `MANAGE_USERS` permission.


> Body parameter

```json
{
  "ids": [
    0
  ],
  "permissions": 0
}
```

```json
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "plexToken": "string",
    "plexUsername": "string",
    "userType": 1,
    "permissions": 0,
    "avatar": "string",
    "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",

]
```

## Create new user

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/user \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "permissions": 0
  }'
```

`POST /user`

Creates a new user. Requires the `MANAGE_USERS` permission.


> Body parameter

```json
{
  "email": "hey@itsme.com",
  "username": "string",
  "permissions": 0
}
```

## Import all users from Plex

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/user/import-from-plex \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "plexIds": [
      "string"
    ]
  }'
```

`POST /user/import-from-plex`

Fetches and imports users from the Plex server. If a list of Plex IDs is provided in the request body, only the specified users will be imported. Otherwise, all users will be imported.

Requires the `MANAGE_USERS` permission.


> Body parameter

```json
{
  "plexIds": [
    "string"
  ]
}
```

<h3 id="import-all-users-from-plex-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|object|false|none|
|» plexIds|body|[string]|false|none|

> Example responses

> 201 Response

```json
[
  {
    "id": 1,
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "plexToken": "string",
    "plexUsername": "string",
    "userType": 1,
    "permissions": 0,
    "avatar": "string",
    "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "requestCount": 5
  }
]
```

<h3 id="import-all-users-from-plex-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|201|Created|A list of the newly created users|Inline|

<h3 id="import-all-users-from-plex-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **201**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|*anonymous*|[[User](#schemauser)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

`POST /user/{userId}/settings/notifications`

```json
      "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z"
    }
  ],
  "playCount": 0
```

<h1 id="overseerr-api-search">search</h1>

## Search for movies, TV shows, or people

> Code samples

```bash
curl 'http://localhost:5055/api/v1/search?query=Mulan' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /search`

Returns a list of movies, TV shows, or people a JSON object.

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|query|query|string|true|none|
|page|query|number|false|none|
|language|query|string|false|none|

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "page": 1,
  "totalPages": 20,
  "totalResults": 200,
  "results": [
    {
      "id": 1234,

      "popularity": 10,

      "backdropPath": "string",
      "voteCount": 0,
      "voteAverage": 0,
      "genreIds": [
        0
      ],
      "overview": "Overview of the movie",
      "originalLanguage": "en",
      "title": "Movie Title",
      "originalTitle": "Original Movie Title",
      "releaseDate": "string",
      "adult": false,
      "video": false,
      "mediaInfo": {
        "id": 0,
        "tmdbId": 0,
        "tvdbId": 0,
        "status": 0,
        "requests": [
          {}
        ],
        "createdAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
        "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Results|Inline|

<h3 id="search-for-movies-tv-shows-or-people-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|page|number|false|none|none|
|totalPages|number|false|none|none|
|totalResults|number|false|none|none|
|results|[[MovieResult](#schemamovieresult) \| [TvResult](#schematvresult) \| [PersonResult](#schemapersonresult)]|false|none|none|
|» *anyOf*|[MovieResult](#schemamovieresult)|false|none|none|
|» *anyOf*|[TvResult](#schematvresult)|false|none|none|
|» *anyOf*|[PersonResult](#schemapersonresult)|false|none|none|

<h1 id="overseerr-api-request">request</h1>

Endpoints related to request management.

## Get all requests

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/request \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /request`

Returns all requests if the user has the `ADMIN` or `MANAGE_REQUESTS` permissions. Otherwise, only the logged-in user's requests are returned.

If the `requestedBy` parameter is specified, only requests from that particular user ID will be returned.


<h3 id="get-all-requests-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|take|query|number \| null|false|none|
|skip|query|number \| null|false|none|
|filter|query|string \| null|false|none|
|sort|query|string|false|none|
|requestedBy|query|number \| null|false|none|

#### Enumerated Values

|Parameter|Value|
|---|---|
|filter|all|
|filter|approved|
|filter|available|
|filter|pending|
|filter|processing|
|filter|unavailable|
|filter|failed|
|filter|deleted|
|filter|completed|
|sort|added|
|sort|modified|

> Example responses

> 200 Response


## Create new request

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/request \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "mediaType": "movie",
    "mediaId": 123,
    "tvdbId": 123,
    "seasons": [
      0
    ],
    "is4k": false,
    "serverId": 0,
    "profileId": 0,
    "rootFolder": "string",
    "languageProfileId": 0,
    "userId": 0
  }'
```

`POST /request`

Creates a new request with the provided media ID and type. The `REQUEST` permission is required.

If the user has the `ADMIN` or `AUTO_APPROVE` permissions, their request will be auomatically approved.


> Body parameter

```json
{
  "mediaType": "movie",
  "mediaId": 123,
  "tvdbId": 123,
  "seasons": [
    0
  ],
  "is4k": false,
  "serverId": 0,
  "profileId": 0,
  "rootFolder": "string",
  "languageProfileId": 0,
  "userId": 0
}
```

<h3 id="create-new-request-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|body|body|object|true|none|
|» mediaType|body|string|true|none|
|» mediaId|body|number|true|none|
|» tvdbId|body|number|false|none|
|» seasons|body|[number] \| string|false|none|
|» » *oneOf*|body|[number]|false|none|
|» » *oneOf*|body|string|false|none|
|» is4k|body|boolean|false|none|
|» serverId|body|number|false|none|
|» profileId|body|number|false|none|
|» rootFolder|body|string|false|none|
|» languageProfileId|body|number|false|none|
|» userId|body|number \| null|false|none|

#### Enumerated Values

|Parameter|Value|
|---|---|
|» mediaType|movie|
|» mediaType|tv|
|» » *oneOf*|all|

> Example responses

> 201 Response

```json
{
  "id": 123,
  "status": 0,
  "media": {},
  "createdAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
  "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
  "requestedBy": {
    "id": 1,
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "plexToken": "string",
    "plexUsername": "string",
    "userType": 1,
    "permissions": 0,
    "avatar": "string",
    "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "requestCount": 5
  },
  "modifiedBy": {
    "id": 1,
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "plexToken": "string",
    "plexUsername": "string",
    "userType": 1,
    "permissions": 0,
    "avatar": "string",
    "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "requestCount": 5
  },
  "is4k": false,
  "serverId": 0,
  "profileId": 0,
  "rootFolder": "string"
}
```

<h3 id="create-new-request-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|201|Created|Succesfully created the request|[MediaRequest](#schemamediarequest)|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Update MediaRequest

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/request/1 \
  -X PUT \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "mediaType": "movie",
    "seasons": [
      0
    ],
    "is4k": false,
    "serverId": 0,
    "profileId": 0,
    "rootFolder": "string",
    "languageProfileId": 0,
    "userId": 0
  }'
```

`PUT /request/{requestId}`

Updates a specific media request and returns the request in a JSON object. Requires the `MANAGE_REQUESTS` permission.

> Body parameter

```json
{
  "mediaType": "movie",
  "seasons": [
    0
  ],
  "is4k": false,
  "serverId": 0,
  "profileId": 0,
  "rootFolder": "string",
  "languageProfileId": 0,
  "userId": 0
}
```

<h3 id="update-mediarequest-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|requestId|path|string|true|Request ID|
|body|body|object|true|none|
|» mediaType|body|string|true|none|
|» seasons|body|[number]|false|none|
|» is4k|body|boolean|false|none|
|» serverId|body|number|false|none|
|» profileId|body|number|false|none|
|» rootFolder|body|string|false|none|
|» languageProfileId|body|number|false|none|
|» userId|body|number \| null|false|none|

#### Enumerated Values

|Parameter|Value|
|---|---|
|» mediaType|movie|
|» mediaType|tv|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "id": 123,
  "status": 0,
  "media": {},

  "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
```

## Delete request

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/request/1 \
  -X DELETE \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`DELETE /request/{requestId}`

Removes a request. If the user has the `MANAGE_REQUESTS` permission, any request can be removed. Otherwise, only pending requests can be removed.

<h3 id="delete-request-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|requestId|path|string|true|Request ID|

<h3 id="delete-request-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|204|No Content|Succesfully removed request|None|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Get similar movies

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/movie/337401/similar \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /movie/{movieId}/similar`

Returns list of similar movies based on the provided movieId in a JSON object.

<h3 id="get-similar-movies-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|movieId|path|number|true|none|
|page|query|number|false|none|
|language|query|string|false|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "page": 1,
  "totalPages": 20,
  "totalResults": 200,
  "results": [
    {
      "id": 1234,
      "mediaType": "string",
      "popularity": 10,
      "posterPath": "string",
      "backdropPath": "string",
      "voteCount": 0,
      "voteAverage": 0,
      "genreIds": [
        0
      ],
      "overview": "Overview of the movie",
      "originalLanguage": "en",
      "title": "Movie Title",
      "originalTitle": "Original Movie Title",
      "releaseDate": "string",
      "adult": false,
      "video": false,
      "mediaInfo": {
        "id": 0,
        "tmdbId": 0,
        "tvdbId": 0,
        "status": 0,
        "requests": [
          {}
        ],
        "createdAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
        "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

<h3 id="get-similar-movies-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|List of movies|Inline|

<h3 id="get-similar-movies-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|page|number|false|none|none|
|totalPages|number|false|none|none|
|totalResults|number|false|none|none|
|results|[[MovieResult](#schemamovieresult)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Get movie ratings

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/movie/337401/ratings \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /movie/{movieId}/ratings`

Returns ratings based on the provided movieId in a JSON object.

<h3 id="get-movie-ratings-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|movieId|path|number|true|none|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "title": "Mulan",
  "year": 2020,
  "url": "http://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/mulan_2020/",
  "criticsScore": 85,
  "criticsRating": "Rotten",
  "audienceScore": 65,
  "audienceRating": "Spilled"
}
```

<h3 id="get-movie-ratings-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Ratings returned|Inline|

<h3 id="get-movie-ratings-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|title|string|false|none|none|
|year|number|false|none|none|
|url|string|false|none|none|
|criticsScore|number|false|none|none|
|criticsRating|string|false|none|none|
|audienceScore|number|false|none|none|
|audienceRating|string|false|none|none|

#### Enumerated Values

|Property|Value|
|---|---|
|criticsRating|Rotten|
|criticsRating|Fresh|
|criticsRating|Certified Fresh|
|audienceRating|Spilled|
|audienceRating|Upright|

To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:

</aside>

|» url|string|false|none|none|
|» criticsScore|number|false|none|none|
|» criticsRating|string|false|none|none|
|» audienceScore|number|false|none|none|
|» audienceRating|string|false|none|none|
|imdb|object|false|none|none|
|» title|string|false|none|none|
|» url|string|false|none|none|
|» criticsScore|number|false|none|none|

|Property|Value|
|---|---|

|» audienceRating|Spilled|
|» audienceRating|Upright|

<h1 id="overseerr-api-media">media</h1>

Endpoints related to media management.

## Get media

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/media \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`GET /media`

Returns all media (can be filtered and limited) in a JSON object.

<h3 id="get-media-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|take|query|number \| null|false|none|
|skip|query|number \| null|false|none|
|filter|query|string \| null|false|none|
|sort|query|string|false|none|

#### Enumerated Values

|Parameter|Value|
|---|---|
|filter|all|
|filter|available|
|filter|partial|
|filter|allavailable|
|filter|processing|
|filter|pending|
|filter|deleted|
|sort|added|
|sort|modified|
|sort|mediaAdded|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "pageInfo": {
    "page": 1,
    "pages": 10,
    "results": 100
  },
  "results": [
    {
      "id": 0,
      "tmdbId": 0,
      "tvdbId": 0,
      "status": 0,
      "requests": [
        {}
      ],
      "createdAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
      "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

<h3 id="get-media-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Returned media|Inline|

<h3 id="get-media-responseschema">Response Schema</h3>

Status Code **200**

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|pageInfo|[PageInfo](#schemapageinfo)|false|none|none|
|results|[[MediaInfo](#schemamediainfo)]|false|none|none|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Delete media item

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/media/1 \
  -X DELETE \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`DELETE /media/{mediaId}`

Removes a media item. The `MANAGE_REQUESTS` permission is required to perform this action.

<h3 id="delete-media-item-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|mediaId|path|string|true|Media ID|

<h3 id="delete-media-item-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|204|No Content|Succesfully removed media item|None|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Update media status

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/media/1/available \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}" \
  -d '{
    "is4k": false
  }'
```

`POST /media/{mediaId}/{status}`

Updates a media item's status and returns the media in JSON format

> Body parameter

```json
{
  "is4k": false
}
```

<h3 id="update-media-status-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|mediaId|path|string|true|Media ID|
|status|path|string|true|New status|
|body|body|object|false|none|
|» is4k|body|boolean|false|none|

#### Enumerated Values

|Parameter|Value|
|---|---|
|status|available|
|status|partial|
|status|processing|
|status|pending|
|status|unknown|
|status|deleted|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "id": 0,
  "tmdbId": 0,
  "tvdbId": 0,
  "status": 0,
  "requests": [
    {}
  ],
  "createdAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
  "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z"
}
```

<h3 id="update-media-status-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Returned media|[MediaInfo](#schemamediainfo)|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Get watch data

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/media/1/watch_data \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

```json
        "plexUsername": "string",

        "permissions": 0,
        "avatar": "string",
        "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
        "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
        "requestCount": 5
      }
    ]
  },
  "data4k": {
    "playCount7Days": 0,
    "playCount30Days": 0,
    "playCount": 0,
    "users": [
      {
        "id": 1,
        "email": "hey@itsme.com",
        "username": "string",
        "plexToken": "string",
        "plexUsername": "string",
        "userType": 1,
        "permissions": 0,
        "avatar": "string",
        "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
        "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
        "requestCount": 5
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Users|Inline|

|Name|Type|Required|Restrictions|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|

|» playCount|number|false|none|none|

|sort|query|string|false|none|
|filter|query|string|false|none|
|requestedBy|query|number \| null|false|none|

|filter|resolved|

## Delete issue

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/issue/1 \
  -X DELETE \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`DELETE /issue/{issueId}`

Removes an issue. If the user has the `MANAGE_ISSUES` permission, any issue can be removed. Otherwise, only a users own issues can be removed.

<h3 id="delete-issue-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|issueId|path|string|true|Issue ID|

<h3 id="delete-issue-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|204|No Content|Succesfully removed issue|None|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>

## Update an issue's status

> Code samples

```bash
curl http://localhost:5055/api/v1/issue/1/open \
  -X POST \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H "Cookie: connect.sid=${API_KEY}"
```

`POST /issue/{issueId}/{status}`

Updates an issue's status to approved or declined. Also returns the issue in a JSON object.

Requires the `MANAGE_ISSUES` permission or `ADMIN`.


<h3 id="update-an-issues-status-parameters">Parameters</h3>

|Name|In|Type|Required|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|issueId|path|string|true|Issue ID|
|status|path|string|true|New status|

#### Enumerated Values

|Parameter|Value|
|---|---|
|status|open|
|status|resolved|

> Example responses

> 200 Response

```json
{
  "id": 1,
  "issueType": 1,
  "media": {
    "id": 0,
    "tmdbId": 0,
    "tvdbId": 0,
    "status": 0,
    "requests": [
      {}
    ],
    "createdAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-12T10:00:27.000Z"
  },
  "createdBy": {
    "id": 1,
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "plexToken": "string",
    "plexUsername": "string",
    "userType": 1,
    "permissions": 0,
    "avatar": "string",
    "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "requestCount": 5
  },
  "modifiedBy": {
    "id": 1,
    "email": "hey@itsme.com",
    "username": "string",
    "plexToken": "string",
    "plexUsername": "string",
    "userType": 1,
    "permissions": 0,
    "avatar": "string",
    "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
    "requestCount": 5
  },
  "comments": [
    {
      "id": 1,
      "user": {
        "id": 1,
        "email": "hey@itsme.com",
        "username": "string",
        "plexToken": "string",
        "plexUsername": "string",
        "userType": 1,
        "permissions": 0,
        "avatar": "string",
        "createdAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
        "updatedAt": "2020-09-02T05:02:23.000Z",
        "requestCount": 5
      },
      "message": "A comment"
    }
  ]
}
```

<h3 id="update-an-issues-status-responses">Responses</h3>

|Status|Meaning|Description|Schema|
|---|---|---|---|
|200|OK|Issue status changed|[Issue](#schemaissue)|

<aside class="warning">
To perform this operation, you must be authenticated by means of one of the following methods:
cookieAuth, apiKey
</aside>
